const TOPICS = {

  ch1: ["第一章 緒論", "範圍：中藥炮製的起源、發展(黃帝內經、神農本草經、雷公炮炙論)與研究方法。 (本章共50題)"],

  ch2: ["第二章 臨床療效", "範圍：炮製是中醫用藥特點，傳統製藥原則(相畏相殺等)與四氣五味、歸經影響。 (本章共50題)"],

  ch3: ["第三章 炮製目的", "範圍：降低毒性、改變藥性、增強療效、矯臭矯味及化學成分水解等變化。 (本章共50題)"],

  ch4: ["第四章 分類及輔料", "範圍：雷公十七法、三類法、五類法，以及酒醋蜜、滑石粉、蛤粉等輔料。 (本章共50題)"],

  ch5: ["第五章 品質與貯藏", "範圍：淨度、水分、灰分、浸出物，以及泛油、發霉、風化等變異與氣調貯藏。 (本章共50題)"],

  ch6: ["第六章 淨選與加工", "範圍：去蘆、去心、去皮殼、去毛(骨碎補/金櫻子)、去瓤(枳殼)、去核等。 (本章共50題)"],

  ch7: ["第七章 飲片切製", "範圍：淋潤、伏潤軟化，直片、斜片、頂刀片等片型原則，以及陰乾法等。 (本章共50題)"],

  ch8: ["第八章 炒法", "範圍：炒黃、炒焦、炒炭(槐花)，麩炒(蒼朮/枳殼)、米炒(斑蝥)、土炒、砂炒(馬錢子)等。 (本章共50題)"],

  ch9: ["第九章 炙法", "範圍：酒炙(大黃)、醋炙(延胡索)、薑炙(厚朴)、蜜炙(甘草)、鹽炙(杜仲)、油炙(淫羊藿)。 (本章共50題)"]

};

let PER = 20;

const sh = a => {

  a = a.slice();

  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {

    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));

    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];

  }

  return a;

};

const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);

const show = id => ["home", "play", "end"].forEach(s => $(s).classList.toggle("hide", s !== id));

let run = null, wrongBank = [], topic = "ch1";

const bk = () => "paozhi-ch-best-" + topic;

function getBest() {

  try { return +localStorage.getItem(bk()) || 0; } catch (e) { return 0; }

}

function bestTxt() {

  const b = getBest();

  $("best").textContent = b ? `${TOPICS[topic][0]}最高分：${b} 分` : "這個主題還沒有成績，先來一輪。";

}

function setTopic(t) {

  topic = t;

  document.querySelectorAll("#seg button").forEach(b => b.setAttribute("aria-checked", String(b.dataset.t === t)));

  $("scope").textContent = TOPICS[t][1];

  bestTxt();

}

bestTxt();

function pickRound() {

  return sh(QUESTIONS_DB[topic]).slice(0, 15);

}

function prep(q) {

  const idx = sh([0, 1, 2, 3]);

  return { ...q, opts: idx.map(i => q.o[i]), ans: idx.indexOf(q.a) };

}

function begin(list, isRetry) {

  PER = 20;

  run = { list: list.map(prep), i: 0, score: 0, log: [], retry: isRetry, t0: 0, timer: null, locked: false };

  show("play");

  ask();

}

function ask() {

  const q = run.list[run.i];

  run.locked = false;

  $("prog").textContent = `第 ${run.i + 1} / ${run.list.length} 題`;

  $("sc").textContent = run.score;

  $("cat").textContent = q.c;

  $("q").textContent = q.q;

  const box = $("opts");

  box.innerHTML = "";

  q.opts.forEach((t, i) => {

    const b = document.createElement("button");

    b.className = "opt";

    b.textContent = t;

    b.onclick = () => pick(i, b);

    box.appendChild(b);

  });

  run.t0 = performance.now();

  clearInterval(run.timer);

  tick();

  run.timer = setInterval(tick, 100);

}

function left() {

  return Math.max(0, PER - (performance.now() - run.t0) / 1000);

}

function tick() {

  const l = left();

  $("tl").textContent = Math.ceil(l);

  $("fill").style.transform = `scaleX(${l / PER})`;

  $("fill").style.background = l > 10 ? "var(--ok)" : l > 5 ? "#F0A04B" : "var(--bad)";

  if (l <= 0) finish(null);

}

function pick(i, b) {

  if (run.locked) return;

  b.classList.add("sel");

  finish(i);

}

function finish(choice) {

  if (run.locked) return;

  run.locked = true;

  clearInterval(run.timer);

  const q = run.list[run.i], l = left(), ok = choice === q.ans;

  const pts = ok ? 100 + Math.round(l / PER * 100) : 0;

  run.score += pts;

  run.log.push({ q, choice, ok, pts });

  $("sc").textContent = run.score;

  setTimeout(() => {

    run.i++;

    run.i < run.list.length ? ask() : results();

  }, choice === null ? 150 : 250);

}

function results() {

  const L = run.log, right = L.filter(x => x.ok).length, max = L.length * 200;

  wrongBank = L.filter(x => !x.ok).map(x => x.q);

  if (!run.retry && run.score > getBest()) {

    try { localStorage.setItem(bk(), run.score); } catch (e) {}

  }

  $("etitle").textContent = run.retry ? "重練成績" : "本輪成績";

  $("esc").textContent = `${run.score} 分`;

  $("esub").textContent = run.retry ? `重練 ${L.length} 題，答對 ${right} 題。` : `滿分 ${max} 分，答對 ${right} / ${L.length} 題。`;

 

  const cats = [...new Set(L.map(x => x.q.c))];

  $("stats").innerHTML = cats.map(c => {

    const a = L.filter(x => x.q.c === c);

    return `<div>${c}<b>${a.length ? a.filter(x => x.ok).length + " / " + a.length : "–"}</b></div>`;

  }).join("");

 

  const rb = $("retry");

  rb.classList.toggle("hide", !wrongBank.length);

  rb.textContent = `重練錯題（${wrongBank.length} 題）`;

 

  $("rev").innerHTML = "";

  L.forEach((x, n) => {

    const d = document.createElement("div");

    d.className = "rv " + (x.ok ? "ok" : "no");

    const mine = x.choice === null ? "（逾時未作答）" : x.q.opts[x.choice];

    d.innerHTML = `<div class="r"><span class="${x.ok ? "ok-t" : "no-t"}">${x.ok ? "答對" : "答錯"}</span> 第 ${n + 1} 題・${x.q.c}・${x.pts} 分</div><div><b></b></div><div class="r"></div><div class="r"></div><div class="r mute" style="margin-top:4px;"></div>`;

    const k = d.children;

    k[1].firstChild.textContent = x.q.q;

    k[2].textContent = "你的答案：" + mine;

    k[3].textContent = "正確答案：" + x.q.opts[x.q.ans];

    k[4].textContent = x.q.e;

    if (x.ok) k[2].textContent = "你的答案：" + mine + "（正確）";

    $("rev").appendChild(d);

  });

 

  bestTxt();

  show("end");

  window.scrollTo(0, 0);

}

$("start").onclick = () => begin(pickRound(), false);

$("again").onclick = () => begin(pickRound(), false);

$("retry").onclick = () => begin(sh(wrongBank), true);

$("home2").onclick = () => show("home");

document.querySelectorAll("#seg button").forEach(b => b.onclick = () => setTopic(b.dataset.t));

setTopic("ch1");

</script>

</body>

</html>
